### [1] [PhyGile: Physics-Prefix Guided Motion Generation for Agile General Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19305)
*Jiacheng Bao,Haoran Yang,Yucheng Xin,Junhong Liu,Yuecheng Xu,Han Liang,Pengfei Han,Xiaoguang Ma,Dong Wang,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhyGile是一个统一框架，通过物理前缀引导的机器人原生运动生成和通用运动跟踪，解决文本到运动生成模型在机器人执行时的物理可行性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到运动生成模型主要基于人类运动数据集训练，其先验假设人类生物力学、驱动、质量分布和接触策略。当这些运动直接重定向到人形机器人时，虽然满足几何约束，但经常违反物理可行性，导致无法在实际机器人上执行。

Method: PhyGile采用统一框架，包含两个阶段：1）训练通用运动跟踪控制器，采用课程式专家混合方案，并在未标记运动数据上进行后训练以提高鲁棒性；2）物理前缀适应阶段，在推理时进行物理前缀引导的机器人原生运动生成，直接在262维骨骼空间中生成机器人原生运动，避免推理时重定向伪影。

Result: 广泛的离线和真实机器人实验表明，PhyGile扩展了文本驱动人形控制的前沿，能够稳定跟踪敏捷、高难度的全身运动，远超先前方法通常实现的行走和低动态运动。

Conclusion: PhyGile通过物理前缀引导的机器人原生运动生成和通用运动跟踪的闭环，有效解决了文本到运动生成在机器人执行时的物理可行性问题，实现了复杂运动在真实机器人上的敏捷稳定执行。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are expected to execute agile and expressive whole-body motions in real-world settings. Existing text-to-motion generation models are predominantly trained on captured human motion datasets, whose priors assume human biomechanics, actuation, mass distribution, and contact strategies. When such motions are directly retargeted to humanoid robots, the resulting trajectories may satisfy geometric constraints (e.g., joint limits and pose continuity) and appear kinematically reasonable. However, they frequently violate the physical feasibility required for real-world execution. To address these issues, we present PhyGile, a unified framework that closes the loop between robot-native motion generation and General Motion Tracking (GMT). PhyGile performs physics-prefix-guided robot-native motion generation at inference time, directly generating robot-native motions in a 262-dimensional skeletal space with physics-guided prefixes, thereby eliminating inference-time retargeting artifacts and reducing generation-execution discrepancies. Before physics-prefix adaptation, we train the GMT controller with a curriculum-based mixture-of-experts scheme, followed by post-training on unlabeled motion data to improve robustness over large-scale robot motions. During physics-prefix adaptation, the GMT controller is further fine-tuned with generated objectives under physics-derived prefixes, enabling agile and stable execution of complex motions on real robots. Extensive offline and real-robot experiments demonstrate that PhyGile expands the frontier of text-driven humanoid control, enabling stable tracking of agile, highly difficult whole-body motions that go well beyond walking and low-dynamic motions typically achieved by prior methods.

</details>


### [2] [VAMPO: Policy Optimization for Improving Visual Dynamics in Video Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19370)
*Zirui Ge,Pengxiang Ding,Baohua Yin,Qishen Wang,Zhiyong Xie,Yemin Wang,Jinbo Wang,Hengtao Li,Runze Suo,Wenxuan Song,Han Zhao,Shangke Lyu,Zhaoxin Fan,Haoang Li,Ran Cheng,Cheng Chi,Huibin Ge,Yaozhi Luo,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAMPO是一个后训练框架，通过策略优化直接改进视频动作模型的视觉动力学，将多步去噪视为序列决策过程，优化去噪策略以提升下游机器人控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散的视频预测器使用似然替代目标训练，鼓励全局合理的预测但未显式优化对操作至关重要的精确视觉动力学（物体姿态、空间关系、接触时机），这种目标不匹配会导致下游策略放大细微错误。

Method: 将多步去噪过程建模为序列决策过程，在潜在空间中基于专家视觉动力学定义奖励函数，引入Euler Hybrid采样器（仅在第一步注入随机性以实现低方差策略梯度估计），结合GRPO和可验证的非对抗性奖励进行优化。

Result: 在多样化的模拟和真实世界操作任务中，VAMPO改善了任务相关的视觉动力学，带来更好的下游动作生成和更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VAMPO通过策略优化框架直接优化视频动作模型的视觉动力学，解决了扩散模型训练目标与机器人控制需求之间的不匹配问题，显著提升了操作任务的性能。

Abstract: Video action models are an appealing foundation for Vision--Language--Action systems because they can learn visual dynamics from large-scale video data and transfer this knowledge to downstream robot control. Yet current diffusion-based video predictors are trained with likelihood-surrogate objectives, which encourage globally plausible predictions without explicitly optimizing the precision-critical visual dynamics needed for manipulation. This objective mismatch often leads to subtle errors in object pose, spatial relations, and contact timing that can be amplified by downstream policies. We propose VAMPO, a post-training framework that directly improves visual dynamics in video action models through policy optimization. Our key idea is to formulate multi-step denoising as a sequential decision process and optimize the denoising policy with rewards defined over expert visual dynamics in latent space. To make this optimization practical, we introduce an Euler Hybrid sampler that injects stochasticity only at the first denoising step, enabling tractable low-variance policy-gradient estimation while preserving the coherence of the remaining denoising trajectory. We further combine this design with GRPO and a verifiable non-adversarial reward. Across diverse simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, VAMPO improves task-relevant visual dynamics, leading to better downstream action generation and stronger generalization. The homepage is https://vampo-robot.github.io/VAMPO/.

</details>


### [3] [A Closed-Form CLF-CBF Controller for Whole-Body Continuum Soft Robot Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19424)
*Kiwan Wong,Maximillian Stölzle,Wei Xiao,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于控制李雅普诺夫函数-控制屏障函数(CLF-CBF)的闭环控制器，用于软连续体机械臂的实时3D避障，无需在线优化，比传统方法快10-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软连续体机械臂通过机械柔顺性提供被动安全性，但仍需主动控制实现可靠避障。现有方法如基于采样的规划计算成本高且缺乏形式化安全保证，难以用于实时全身避障。

Method: 提出闭式CLF-CBF控制器，通过解析方式将安全约束嵌入控制输入，确保稳定性和安全性，避免在线优化方法的可行性问题。

Result: 控制器比标准CLF-CBF二次规划方法快10倍，比传统采样规划器快100倍。在肌腱驱动软机械臂上的仿真和硬件实验展示了精确的3D轨迹跟踪和复杂环境中的鲁棒避障。

Conclusion: 该框架为在动态、安全关键环境中运行的软机器人提供了可扩展且可证明安全的控制策略。

Abstract: Safe operation is essential for deploying robots in human-centered 3D environments. Soft continuum manipulators provide passive safety through mechanical compliance, but still require active control to achieve reliable collision avoidance. Existing approaches, such as sampling-based planning, are often computationally expensive and lack formal safety guarantees, which limits their use for real-time whole-body avoidance.
  This paper presents a closed-form Control Lyapunov Function--Control Barrier Function (CLF--CBF) controller for real-time 3D obstacle avoidance in soft continuum manipulators without online optimization. By analytically embedding safety constraints into the control input, the proposed method ensures stability and safety under the stated modeling assumptions, while avoiding feasibility issues commonly encountered in online optimization-based methods. The resulting controller is up to $10\times$ faster than standard CLF--CBF quadratic-programming approaches and up to $100\times$ faster than traditional sampling-based planners.
  Simulation and hardware experiments on a tendon-driven soft manipulator demonstrate accurate 3D trajectory tracking and robust obstacle avoidance in cluttered environments. These results show that the proposed framework provides a scalable and provably safe control strategy for soft robots operating in dynamic, safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond detection: cooperative multi-agent reasoning for rapid onboard EO crisis response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19858)
*Alejandro D. Mousist,Pedro Delgado de Robles Martín,Raquel Lladró Climent,Julian Cobos Aparicio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于星上地球观测处理的分层多智能体架构，在资源受限条件下通过事件驱动决策管道协调专用AI智能体，实现快速灾害事件识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前地球观测监测管道主要依赖地面处理，存在下行链路限制、多源数据融合约束和全场景分析计算成本高等延迟问题，需要实现星上快速灾害事件识别以支持下一代自主EO任务。

Method: 采用分层多智能体架构，包括早期预警智能体（从星上观测生成快速假设并选择性激活领域特定分析智能体）和决策智能体（整合证据发出最终警报）。结合视觉语言模型、传统遥感分析工具和角色专用智能体，在分布式节点（如卫星平台）上实现结构化多模态观测推理。

Result: 在已部署在轨的边缘计算平台工程模型上进行了概念验证，使用代表性卫星数据。在野火和洪水监测场景实验中，基于路由的管道显著降低了计算开销，同时保持一致的决策输出。

Conclusion: 该架构证明了分布式基于智能体的推理对未来自主地球观测星座的可行性，能够在严格资源和带宽约束下实现快速灾害事件识别，减少不必要的计算。

Abstract: Rapid identification of hazardous events is essential for next-generation Earth Observation (EO) missions supporting disaster response. However, current monitoring pipelines remain largely ground-centric, introducing latency due to downlink limitations, multi-source data fusion constraints, and the computational cost of exhaustive scene analysis.
  This work proposes a hierarchical multi-agent architecture for onboard EO processing under strict resource and bandwidth constraints. The system enables the exploitation of complementary multimodal observations by coordinating specialized AI agents within an event-driven decision pipeline. AI agents can be deployed across multiple nodes in a distributed setting, such as satellite platforms. An Early Warning agent generates fast hypotheses from onboard observations and selectively activates domain-specific analysis agents, while a Decision agent consolidates the evidence to issue a final alert. The architecture combines vision-language models, traditional remote sensing analysis tools, and role-specialized agents to enable structured reasoning over multimodal observations while minimizing unnecessary computation.
  A proof-of-concept implementation was executed on the engineering model of an edge-computing platform currently deployed in orbit, using representative satellite data. Experiments on wildfire and flood monitoring scenarios show that the proposed routing-based pipeline significantly reduces computational overhead while maintaining coherent decision outputs, demonstrating the feasibility of distributed agent-based reasoning for future autonomous EO constellations.

</details>


### [5] [Can LLMs Prove Robotic Path Planning Optimality? A Benchmark for Research-Level Algorithm Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19464)
*Zhengbang Yang,Md. Tasin Tazwar,Minghan Wei,Zhuangdi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了首个评估大语言模型在机器人路径规划算法近似比证明能力上的基准测试，包含34个研究级证明任务，发现当前LLMs难以独立完成有效证明，但通过提供任务特定引理可显著提升推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人路径规划问题通常是NP难的，实际解决方案依赖于具有理论性能保证的近似算法。设计这类算法具有挑战性，而形式化证明其近似最优性更为困难，需要领域特定的几何洞察力和对复杂操作约束的多步数学推理。尽管最近的大语言模型在数学推理基准测试中表现出色，但它们在机器人路径规划研究级最优性证明方面的能力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 创建首个针对机器人路径规划算法近似比证明的LLM评估基准，包含34个研究级证明任务，涵盖不同规划问题类型和复杂度级别；2. 评估最先进的专有和开源LLMs；3. 比较不同提示策略：任务特定引理、通用思维链提示、提供真实近似比作为后验知识；4. 进行细粒度错误分析，识别常见逻辑错误和幻觉；5. 展示如何通过针对性上下文增强来缓解每种错误类型。

Result: 1. 即使最强的模型在没有外部领域知识的情况下也难以产生完全有效的证明；2. 为LLMs提供任务特定的上下文引理可显著提高推理质量，这比通用思维链提示或提供真实近似比作为后验知识更有效；3. 细粒度错误分析揭示了常见的逻辑失败和幻觉模式；4. 通过针对性上下文增强可以缓解每种错误类型。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了LLMs在机器人路径规划算法理论证明能力评估方面的空白，证明了任务特定领域知识对提升LLMs推理质量的重要性，为未来开发更有效的AI辅助证明系统提供了基准和方法指导。

Abstract: Robotic path planning problems are often NP-hard, and practical solutions typically rely on approximation algorithms with provable performance guarantees for general cases. While designing such algorithms is challenging, formally proving their approximation optimality is even more demanding, which requires domain-specific geometric insights and multi-step mathematical reasoning over complex operational constraints. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, yet their ability to assist with research-level optimality proofs in robotic path planning remains under-explored. In this work, we introduce the first benchmark for evaluating LLMs on approximation-ratio proofs of robotic path planning algorithms. The benchmark consists of 34 research-grade proof tasks spanning diverse planning problem types and complexity levels, each requiring structured reasoning over algorithm descriptions, problem constraints, and theoretical guarantees. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source LLMs reveals that even the strongest models struggle to produce fully valid proofs without external domain knowledge. However, providing LLMs with task-specific in-context lemmas substantially improves reasoning quality, a factor that is more effective than generic chain-of-thought prompting or supplying the ground-truth approximation ratio as posterior knowledge. We further provide fine-grained error analysis to characterize common logical failures and hallucinations, and demonstrate how each error type can be mitigated through targeted context augmentation.

</details>


### [6] [IndoorR2X: Indoor Robot-to-Everything Coordination with LLM-Driven Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20182)
*Fan Yang,Soumya Teotia,Shaunak A. Mehta,Prajit KrisshnaKumar,Quanting Xie,Jun Liu,Yueqi Song,Li Wenkai,Atsunori Moteki,Kanji Uchino,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IndoorR2X：首个用于室内环境中基于LLM的多机器人任务规划与Robot-to-Everything感知通信的基准和仿真框架，通过整合移动机器人和静态IoT设备观测构建全局语义状态，提升场景理解并减少冗余探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人间通信(R2R)在室内场景理解中存在局限性，无法克服部分可观测性且需要大量探索开销或扩大团队规模。而室内环境中已有的低成本IoT传感器（如摄像头）能提供超越机载感知的持久、建筑级上下文信息。

Method: 提出IndoorR2X框架，整合移动机器人和静态IoT设备的观测构建全局语义状态，支持基于LLM的高层任务规划。提供可配置的仿真环境、传感器布局、机器人团队和任务套件，系统评估高层语义协调策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，IoT增强的世界建模提高了多机器人系统的效率和可靠性。研究突出了基于LLM的机器人团队与室内IoT传感器协作的关键见解和失败模式。

Conclusion: IndoorR2X为评估LLM驱动的多机器人任务规划与R2X感知通信提供了首个基准框架，展示了IoT增强感知在提升室内多机器人协作效率方面的潜力，为未来研究提供了系统评估工具。

Abstract: Although robot-to-robot (R2R) communication improves indoor scene understanding beyond what a single robot can achieve, R2R alone cannot overcome partial observability without substantial exploration overhead or scaling team size. In contrast, many indoor environments already include low-cost Internet of Things (IoT) sensors (e.g., cameras) that provide persistent, building-wide context beyond onboard perception. We therefore introduce IndoorR2X, the first benchmark and simulation framework for Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multi-robot task planning with Robot-to-Everything (R2X) perception and communication in indoor environments. IndoorR2X integrates observations from mobile robots and static IoT devices to construct a global semantic state that supports scalable scene understanding, reduces redundant exploration, and enables high-level coordination through LLM-based planning. IndoorR2X provides configurable simulation environments, sensor layouts, robot teams, and task suites to systematically evaluate high-level semantic coordination strategies. Extensive experiments across diverse settings demonstrate that IoT-augmented world modeling improves multi-robot efficiency and reliability, and we highlight key insights and failure modes for advancing LLM-based collaboration between robot teams and indoor IoT sensors.

</details>


### [7] [Real-Time Optical Communication Using Event-Based Vision with Moving Transmitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19477)
*Harmeet Dhillon,Pranay Katyal,Brendan Long,Rohan Walia,Matthew Cleaveland,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于事件相机的多机器人光学通信系统，通过几何感知无迹卡尔曼滤波器实现移动发射器跟踪和实时消息解码，在运动状态下文本传输准确率超过95%，处理速度比现有方法快7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统射频通信在多机器人系统中存在争用和干扰问题，而基于帧的传统相机存在帧率限制、运动模糊和高动态范围光照下的鲁棒性问题。事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率和高动态范围，对快速相对运动下的场景变化极为敏感，适合用于光学通信。

Method: 开发了完整的光学通信系统，采用几何感知无迹卡尔曼滤波器（GA-UKF）来跟踪移动发射器并实时解码消息。系统利用事件相机的高时间分辨率特性，在快速相对运动条件下实现精确跟踪。

Result: 系统在运动状态下文本传输的解码准确率超过95%，处理速度比现有最先进方法快7倍，在发射频率≥1kHz时保持等效的跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 事件相机为多机器人系统中的光学通信提供了有效解决方案，克服了传统射频通信和帧式相机的局限性，实现了在动态环境下的可靠通信。

Abstract: In multi-robot systems, traditional radio frequency (RF) communication struggles with contention and jamming. Optical communication offers a strong alternative. However, conventional frame-based cameras suffer from limited frame rates, motion blur, and reduced robustness under high dynamic range lighting. Event cameras support microsecond temporal resolution and high dynamic range, making them extremely sensitive to scene changes under fast relative motion with an optical transmitter. Leveraging these strengths, we develop a complete optical communication system capable of tracking moving transmitters and decoding messages in real time. Our system achieves over $95\%$ decoding accuracy for text transmission during motion by implementing a Geometry-Aware Unscented Kalman Filter (GA-UKF), achieving 7x faster processing speed compared to the previous state-of-the-art method, while maintaining equivalent tracking accuracy at transmitting frequencies $\geq$ 1 kHz.

</details>


### [8] [CeRLP: A Cross-embodiment Robot Local Planning Framework for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19602)
*Haoyu Xi,Mingao Tan,Xinming Zhang,Siwei Cheng,Shanze Wang,Yin Gu,Xiaoyu Shen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CeRLP是一个跨具身机器人局部规划框架，通过深度估计尺度校正和视觉到激光扫描的抽象，实现异构机器人间的通用视觉导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨具身机器人视觉导航面临机器人配置和相机参数差异的挑战，现有方法需要大量数据收集或耗时微调，且缺乏对机器人几何结构的显式考虑。

Method: 提出CeRLP框架：1) 深度估计尺度校正方法，通过离线预校准解决单目深度估计的尺度模糊问题；2) 视觉到激光扫描抽象模块，将不同视觉输入投影为高度自适应的激光扫描，使策略对异构机器人具有鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真实验显示CeRLP优于对比方法，验证了其作为局部规划器的鲁棒避障能力。真实世界实验在点对点导航和视觉语言导航任务中验证了有效性，证明了其在不同机器人相机配置下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CeRLP通过统一的几何表示和尺度校正机制，实现了跨异构机器人配置的通用视觉导航，解决了现有方法数据密集、耗时且缺乏几何考虑的问题。

Abstract: Visual navigation for cross-embodiment robots is challenging due to variations in robot and camera configurations, which can lead to the failure of navigation tasks. Previous approaches typically rely on collecting massive datasets across different robots, which is highly data-intensive, or fine-tuning models, which is time-consuming. Furthermore, both methods often lack explicit consideration of robot geometry. In this paper, we propose a Cross-embodiment Robot Local Planning (CeRLP) framework for general visual navigation, which abstracts visual information into a unified geometric formulation and applies to heterogeneous robots with varying physical dimensions, camera parameters, and camera types. CeRLP introduces a depth estimation scale correction method that utilizes offline pre-calibration to resolve the scale ambiguity of monocular depth estimation, thereby recovering precise metric depth images. Furthermore, CeRLP designs a visual-to-scan abstraction module that projects varying visual inputs into height-adaptive laser scans, making the policy robust to heterogeneous robots. Experiments in simulation environments demonstrate that CeRLP outperforms comparative methods, validating its robust obstacle avoidance capabilities as a local planner. Additionally, extensive real-world experiments verify the effectiveness of CeRLP in tasks such as point-to-point navigation and vision-language navigation, demonstrating its generalization across varying robot and camera configurations.

</details>


### [9] [Legged Autonomous Surface Science In Analogue Environments (LASSIE): Making Every Robotic Step Count in Planetary Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19661)
*Cristina G. Wilson,Marion Nachon,Shipeng Liu,John G. Ruck,J. Diego Caporale,Benjamin E. McKeeby,Yifeng Zhang,Jordan M. Bretzfelder,John Bush,Alivia M. Eng,Ethan Fulcher,Emmy B. Hughes,Ian C. Rankin,Jelis J. Sostre Cortés,Sophie Silver,Michael R. Zanetti,Ryan C. Ewing,Kenton R. Fisher,Douglas J. Jerolmack,Daniel E. Koditschek,Frances Rivera-Hernández,Thomas F. Shipley,Feifei Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种新方法增强行星表面探索能力：使用腿式机器人测量土壤力学特性以穿越复杂地形，以及采用人类启发式算法实现自适应数据采集决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前行星表面探索受限于轮式机器人在复杂地形中的移动能力，以及预编程数据采集计划缺乏现场灵活性，需要更高效的探索方法。

Method: 1) 高机动性腿式机器人通过直接表面交互收集地形力学信息；2) 人类启发式数据采集算法，使机器人能够基于科学假设和地面传感测量调整探索优先级。

Result: 在实验室和两个行星模拟环境中成功验证：腿式机器人测量土壤力学特性有效支持复杂地形穿越；土壤力学测量结合其他地质特性揭示了地质环境形成的关键因素；人类启发式算法使机器人成为科学家的队友，支持更灵活的自适应数据采集。

Conclusion: 该方法通过集成人机系统，能够探索更广泛的行星环境并提供新的基质调查机会，实现最大科学回报。

Abstract: The ability to efficiently and effectively explore planetary surfaces is currently limited by the capability of wheeled rovers to traverse challenging terrains, and by pre-programmed data acquisition plans with limited in-situ flexibility. In this paper, we present two novel approaches to address these limitations: (i) high-mobility legged robots that use direct surface interactions to collect rich information about the terrain's mechanics to guide exploration; (ii) human-inspired data acquisition algorithms that enable robots to reason about scientific hypotheses and adapt exploration priorities based on incoming ground-sensing measurements. We successfully verify our approach through lab work and field deployments in two planetary analog environments. The new capability for legged robots to measure soil mechanical properties is shown to enable effective traversal of challenging terrains. When coupled with other geologic properties (e.g., composition, thermal properties, and grain size data etc), soil mechanical measurements reveal key factors governing the formation and development of geologic environments. We then demonstrate how human-inspired algorithms turn terrain-sensing robots into teammates, by supporting more flexible and adaptive data collection decisions with human scientists. Our approach therefore enables exploration of a wider range of planetary environments and new substrate investigation opportunities through integrated human-robot systems that support maximum scientific return.

</details>


### [10] [Real-Time Structural Detection for Indoor Navigation from 3D LiDAR Using Bird's-Eye-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19830)
*Guanliang Li,Pedro Espinosa Angulo,David Perez Saura,Santiago Tapia Fernandez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 轻量级实时框架将3D LiDAR数据投影到2D鸟瞰图，评估多种特征提取方法，YOLO-OBB在资源受限平台上实现最佳鲁棒性与计算效率平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限机器人需要高效的结构感知用于建图和自主导航，现有3D方法计算成本过高，传统2D几何方法缺乏鲁棒性

Method: 将3D LiDAR数据投影到2D鸟瞰图，系统评估多种特征提取策略（霍夫变换、RANSAC、LSD等经典几何方法和基于YOLO-OBB的深度学习检测器），通过时空融合模块整合检测结果

Result: 经典方法（霍夫和LSD）响应快但对噪声敏感，LSD产生过多分段碎片导致系统拥塞；RANSAC鲁棒性更好但无法满足实时约束；YOLO-OBB方法在鲁棒性和计算效率间达到最佳平衡，在低功耗单板计算机上满足10Hz实时操作

Conclusion: 提出了一种计算高效的基于BEV的感知流水线，能够在资源受限的机器人平台上实现可靠的实时结构检测，无需GPU加速处理

Abstract: Efficient structural perception is essential for mapping and autonomous navigation on resource-constrained robots. Existing 3D methods are computationally prohibitive, while traditional 2D geometric approaches lack robustness. This paper presents a lightweight, real-time framework that projects 3D LiDAR data into 2D Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) images to enable efficient detection of structural elements relevant to mapping and navigation. Within this representation, we systematically evaluate several feature extraction strategies, including classical geometric techniques (Hough Transform, RANSAC, and LSD) and a deep learning detector based on YOLO-OBB. The resulting detections are integrated through a spatiotemporal fusion module that improves stability and robustness across consecutive frames. Experiments conducted on a standard mobile robotic platform highlight clear performance trade-offs. Classical methods such as Hough and LSD provide fast responses but exhibit strong sensitivity to noise, with LSD producing excessive segment fragmentation that leads to system congestion. RANSAC offers improved robustness but fails to meet real-time constraints. In contrast, the YOLO-OBB-based approach achieves the best balance between robustness and computational efficiency, maintaining an end-to-end latency (satisfying 10 Hz operation) while effectively filtering cluttered observations in a low-power single-board computer (SBC) without using GPU acceleration. The main contribution of this work is a computationally efficient BEV-based perception pipeline enabling reliable real-time structural detection from 3D LiDAR on resource-constrained robotic platforms that cannot rely on GPU-intensive processing.

</details>


### [11] [Multi-Agent Motion Planning on Industrial Magnetic Levitation Platforms: A Hybrid ADMM-HOCBF approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19838)
*Bavo Tistaert,Stan Servaes,Alejandro Gonzalez-Garcia,Ibrahim Ibrahim,Louis Callens,Jan Swevers,Wilm Decré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于完整多智能体系统的混合运动规划方法，结合去中心化ADMM与集中式高阶控制屏障函数，在保证安全性的同时提高可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式模型预测控制在智能体数量增加时计算复杂度急剧上升，难以实现实时控制。需要一种既能保证安全性又具有良好可扩展性的去中心化控制框架。

Method: 提出去中心化MPC框架，结合交替方向乘子法的去中心化版本与集中式高阶控制屏障函数架构。使用高效C++实现，并在真实工业磁悬浮平台上部署验证。

Result: 仿真结果显示相比传统集中式MPC在可扩展性方面有显著提升。方法在实际工业磁悬浮平台上验证了有效性和实时能力。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了多智能体系统运动规划中的可扩展性问题，在保证安全性的同时实现了去中心化实时控制，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel hybrid motion planning method for holonomic multi-agent systems. The proposed decentralised model predictive control (MPC) framework tackles the intractability of classical centralised MPC for a growing number of agents while providing safety guarantees. This is achieved by combining a decentralised version of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) with a centralised high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) architecture. Simulation results show significant improvement in scalability over classical centralised MPC. We validate the efficacy and real-time capability of the proposed method by developing a highly efficient C++ implementation and deploying the resulting trajectories on a real industrial magnetic levitation platform.

</details>


### [12] ["You've got a friend in me": Co-Designing a Peer Social Robot for Young Newcomers' Language and Cultural Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18804)
*Neil Fernandes,Cheng Tang,Tehniyat Shahbaz,Alex Hauschildt,Emily Davies-Robinson,Yue Hu,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文介绍了Maple，一款专为加拿大新移民儿童设计的桌面社交辅助机器人，用于在社区扫盲项目中作为语言练习伙伴，解决师资不足和个性化支持有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大社区扫盲项目面临师资有限、一对一辅导时间稀缺的问题，这限制了为新移民儿童提供个性化的英语和文化学习支持。需要创新的解决方案来增强辅导效果。

Method: 采用协同设计研究方法，与United for Literacy的导师合作，通过观察和访谈确定新移民儿童的具体需求，设计开发了Maple桌面社交辅助机器人原型。系统整合了短篇故事活动、多模态支架（语音、面部反馈、手势）和嵌入式测验等功能。

Result: 开发了Maple原型系统，该系统能够支持注意力集中，同时为导师提供可操作的形成性信号。研究提出了在社区环境中支持语言社会化的导师参与式社交辅助机器人系统设计启示。

Conclusion: 论文为社区环境中支持语言社会化的导师参与式社交辅助机器人系统提供了设计启示，并规划了在真实项目中以儿童为中心的评价方向，为解决新移民儿童语言学习支持不足的问题提供了创新方案。

Abstract: Community literacy programs supporting young newcomer children in Canada face limited staffing and scarce one-to-one time, which constrains personalized English and cultural learning support. This paper reports on a co-design study with United for Literacy tutors that informed Maple, a table-top, peer-like Socially Assistive Robot (SAR) designed as a practice partner within tutor-mediated sessions. From shadowing and co-design interviews, we derived newcomer-specific requirements and added them in an integrated prototype that uses short story-based activities, multi-modal scaffolding (speech, facial feedback, gesture), and embedded quizzes that support attention while producing tutor-actionable formative signals. We contribute system design implications for tutor-in-the-loop SARs supporting language socialization in community settings and outline directions for child-centered evaluation in authentic programs.

</details>


### [13] [Radar-Inertial Odometry with Online Spatio-Temporal Calibration via Continuous-Time IMU Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19958)
*Vlaho-Josip Štironja,Luka Petrović,Juraj Peršić,Ivan Marković,Ivan Petrović*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种雷达-惯性里程计框架，通过连续时间建模联合在线估计空间和时间校准参数，无需依赖扫描匹配或环境特定假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-惯性里程计方法通常假设已知雷达-IMU外参标定，或依赖充分运动激励进行在线外参估计，且常忽略传感器间的时间不对齐问题。需要一种能同时处理空间和时间校准的鲁棒方法。

Method: 基于因子图优化框架，使用均匀三次B样条对惯性测量进行连续时间建模，准确捕捉雷达-IMU测量的异步特性，实现时间偏移和外参标定参数的联合在线估计。

Result: 提出的连续时间加速度和角速度表示能够可靠收敛时间偏移和外参标定参数，无需扫描匹配、目标跟踪或环境特定假设。

Conclusion: 该框架在具有挑战性的条件下（如低光照、雾、特征缺失环境或恶劣天气）为雷达-惯性里程计提供了鲁棒的替代方案，通过联合时空校准提高了系统可靠性。

Abstract: Radar-Inertial Odometry (RIO) has emerged as a robust alternative to vision- and LiDAR-based odometry in challenging conditions such as low light, fog, featureless environments, or in adverse weather. However, many existing RIO approaches assume known radar-IMU extrinsic calibration or rely on sufficient motion excitation for online extrinsic estimation, while temporal misalignment between sensors is often neglected or treated independently. In this work, we present a RIO framework that performs joint online spatial and temporal calibration within a factor-graph optimization formulation, based on continuous-time modeling of inertial measurements using uniform cubic B-splines. The proposed continuous-time representation of acceleration and angular velocity accurately captures the asynchronous nature of radar-IMU measurements, enabling reliable convergence of both the temporal offset and extrinsic calibration parameters, without relying on scan matching, target tracking, or environment-specific assumptions.

</details>


### [14] [GustPilot: A Hierarchical DRL-INDI Framework for Wind-Resilient Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19966)
*Amir Atef Habel,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Fawad Mehboob,Clement Fortin,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GustPilot：一种结合深度强化学习（DRL）规划与增量非线性动态逆（INDI）控制的分层导航框架，用于提升轻型四旋翼无人机在风扰环境下的自主导航鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风扰是轻型四旋翼无人机可靠自主导航的主要障碍，快速变化的气流会破坏规划与跟踪的稳定性。现有方法在动态风扰环境下表现不佳，需要一种既能进行风感知规划又能快速抑制扰动的解决方案。

Method: 提出分层导航框架GustPilot：1）上层使用DRL策略在惯性系中生成穿越门的速度参考指令，通过风扇-射流域随机化训练实现风感知规划；2）下层采用几何INDI控制器进行低层跟踪，利用机载传感器测量对特定线性加速度和角加速度率提供增量反馈，实现快速残差扰动抑制。

Result: 在50g四旋翼平台上进行真实飞行测试，相比DRL-PID基线：平均总体成功率从55.0%提升至94.7%；跟踪RMSE降低达50%；在3.5 m/s风扰下维持速度达1.34 m/s。策略仅用单门单风扇训练即可泛化到更复杂环境（最多6门4风扇）。

Conclusion: DRL速度规划与结构化INDI扰动抑制相结合，为风扰环境下的自主飞行导航提供了一种实用且可泛化的方法，显著提升了轻型无人机在动态风扰条件下的导航鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Wind disturbances remain a key barrier to reliable autonomous navigation for lightweight quadrotors, where the rapidly varying airflow can destabilize both planning and tracking. This paper introduces GustPilot, a hierarchical wind-resilient navigation stack in which a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) policy generates inertial-frame velocity reference for gate traversal. At the same time, a geometric Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) controller provides low-level tracking with fast residual disturbance rejection. The INDI layer achieves this by providing incremental feedback on both specific linear acceleration and angular acceleration rate, using onboard sensor measurements to reject wind disturbances rapidly. Robustness is obtained through a two-level strategy, wind-aware planning learned via fan-jet domain randomization during training, and rapid execution-time disturbance rejection by the INDI tracking controller. We evaluate GustPilot in real flights on a 50g quad-copter platform against a DRL-PID baseline across four scenarios ranging from no-wind to fully dynamic conditions with a moving gate and a moving disturbance source. Despite being trained only in a minimal single-gate and single-fan setup, the policy generalizes to significantly more complex environments (up to six gates and four fans) without retraining. Across 80 experiments, DRL-INDI achieves a 94.7% versus 55.0% for DRL-PID as average Overall Success Rate (OSR), reduces tracking RMSE up to 50%, and sustains speeds up to 1.34 m/s under wind disturbances up to 3.5 m/s. These results demonstrate that combining DRL-based velocity planning with structured INDI disturbance rejection provides a practical and generalizable approach to wind-resilient autonomous flight navigation.

</details>


### [15] [Uncertainty Matters: Structured Probabilistic Online Mapping for Motion Prediction in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20076)
*Pritom Gogoi,Faris Janjoš,Bin Yang,Andreas Look*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结构化概率公式用于在线地图生成，通过低秩加对角协方差分解建模元素内依赖关系，在nuScenes数据集上优于确定性基线并提升基于地图的运动预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量化地图模型通常将地图估计视为确定性任务，忽略了结构不确定性。现有概率方法常依赖对角协方差矩阵，假设点之间独立，无法捕捉道路几何固有的强空间相关性。

Method: 提出结构化概率公式用于在线地图生成，通过预测密集协方差矩阵显式建模元素内依赖关系，采用低秩加对角协方差分解参数化，将不确定性表示为低秩分量（捕捉全局空间结构）和对角分量（表示独立局部噪声）的组合。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上的评估表明，该不确定性感知框架相比确定性基线在在线地图生成质量上获得一致改进。同时，该方法为基于地图的运动预测建立了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 结构化概率方法能有效捕捉道路几何的空间相关性，在计算成本可控的情况下提升地图生成质量和运动预测性能，凸显了不确定性在规划任务中的关键作用。

Abstract: Online map generation and trajectory prediction are critical components of the autonomous driving perception-prediction-planning pipeline. While modern vectorized mapping models achieve high geometric accuracy, they typically treat map estimation as a deterministic task, discarding structural uncertainty. Existing probabilistic approaches often rely on diagonal covariance matrices, which assume independence between points and fail to capture the strong spatial correlations inherent in road geometry. To address this, we propose a structured probabilistic formulation for online map generation. Our method explicitly models intra-element dependencies by predicting a dense covariance matrix, parameterized via a Low-Rank plus Diagonal (LRPD) covariance decomposition. This formulation represents uncertainty as a combination of a low-rank component, which captures global spatial structure, and a diagonal component representing independent local noise, thereby capturing geometric correlations without the prohibitive computational cost of full covariance matrices. Evaluations on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that our uncertainty-aware framework yields consistent improvements in online map generation quality compared to deterministic baselines. Furthermore, our approach establishes new state-of-the-art performance for map-based motion prediction, highlighting the critical role of uncertainty in planning tasks. Code is published under link-available-soon.

</details>


### [16] [Not an Obstacle for Dog, but a Hazard for Human: A Co-Ego Navigation System for Guide Dog Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20121)
*Ruiping Liu,Jingqi Zhang,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Peter Seungjune Lee,Di Wen,Kunyu Peng,Jiaming Zhang,Kailun Yang,Katja Mombaur,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Co-Ego系统，通过融合机器人地面视角和用户身高视角来解决四足机器人导盲犬导航中的视角不对称问题，显著提高了避障安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导盲犬数量有限，无法满足大多数视障人士需求。四足机器人导盲犬是潜在替代方案，但现有系统仅依赖机器人地面传感器，无法检测对机器人透明但对人体高度危险的障碍物（如弯曲树枝），即视角不对称问题。

Method: 提出Co-Ego系统，采用双分支避障框架：1) 机器人中心地面感知分支；2) 用户身高视角的自我中心感知分支。系统部署在四足机器人上，通过跨视角融合实现全面导航安全。

Result: 在受控用户研究中，对比无辅助、单视角和跨视角融合三种条件。结果显示跨视角融合显著减少碰撞次数和认知负荷，验证了视角互补对安全机器人导盲犬导航的必要性。

Conclusion: 视角不对称是机器人导盲犬导航的关键挑战，Co-Ego系统通过融合机器人地面视角和用户身高视角，首次明确解决了这一问题，为安全导航提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Guide dogs offer independence to Blind and Low-Vision (BLV) individuals, yet their limited availability leaves the vast majority of BLV users without access. Quadruped robotic guide dogs present a promising alternative, but existing systems rely solely on the robot's ground-level sensors for navigation, overlooking a critical class of hazards: obstacles that are transparent to the robot yet dangerous at human body height, such as bent branches. We term this the viewpoint asymmetry problem and present the first system to explicitly address it. Our Co-Ego system adopts a dual-branch obstacle avoidance framework that integrates the robot-centric ground sensing with the user's elevated egocentric perspective to ensure comprehensive navigation safety. Deployed on a quadruped robot, the system is evaluated in a controlled user study with sighted participants under blindfold across three conditions: unassisted, single-view, and cross-view fusion. Results demonstrate that cross-view fusion significantly reduces collision times and cognitive load, verifying the necessity of viewpoint complementarity for safe robotic guide dog navigation.

</details>


### [17] [KUKAloha: A General, Low-Cost, and Shared-Control based Teleoperation Framework for Construction Robot Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20129)
*Yifan Xu,Qizhang Shen,Vineet Kamat,Carol Menassa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KUKAloha是一个低成本、共享控制的遥操作框架，用于建筑机器人手臂，通过领导者-跟随者范式实现直观的粗调控制与自主精确定位的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个通用、低成本、共享控制的遥操作框架，用于建筑机器人手臂，旨在提高大型机械臂操作的安全性、可重复性和实用性。

Method: 采用领导者-跟随者范式：轻量级领导臂实现直观的人机引导（粗调运动），基于AprilTag检测的自主感知模块执行精确定位和抓取操作，将人工控制与精细操作解耦。

Result: 在KUKA机器人手臂上实现该框架，并通过代表性建筑操作任务进行可用性研究。实验结果表明，KUKAloha减少了操作员工作量，提高了任务完成效率，为建筑环境中的可扩展演示收集和共享人机控制提供了实用解决方案。

Conclusion: KUKAloha框架通过解耦人工控制与精细操作，提高了大型机械臂操作的安全性和可重复性，为建筑环境中的机器人遥操作提供了有效的共享控制解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents KUKAloha, a general, low-cost, and shared-control teleoperation framework designed for construction robot arms. The proposed system employs a leader-follower paradigm in which a lightweight leading arm enables intuitive human guidance for coarse robot motion, while an autonomous perception module based on AprilTag detection performs precise alignment and grasp execution. By explicitly decoupling human control from fine manipulation, KUKAloha improves safety and repeatability when operating large-scale manipulators. We implement the framework on a KUKA robot arm and conduct a usability study with representative construction manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that KUKAloha reduces operator workload, improves task completion efficiency, and provides a practical solution for scalable demonstration collection and shared human-robot control in construction environments.

</details>


### [18] [AGILE: A Comprehensive Workflow for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20147)
*Huihua Zhao,Rafael Cathomen,Lionel Gulich,Wei Liu,Efe Arda Ongan,Michael Lin,Shalin Jain,Soha Pouya,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AGILE是一个用于人形机器人强化学习的端到端工作流，通过标准化策略开发生命周期来解决仿真到现实转移的挑战，在多个硬件平台上验证了五种代表性技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习在仿真中实现了令人印象深刻的人形机器人行为，但将这些结果转移到新机器人仍然具有挑战性。实际部署中的主要瓶颈不再是仿真吞吐量或算法设计，而是缺乏将环境验证、训练、评估和部署连接成连贯循环的系统化基础设施。

Method: AGILE包含四个阶段：(1)交互式环境验证，(2)可重复训练，(3)统一评估，(4)通过机器人/任务配置描述符驱动的部署。在评估阶段支持基于场景的测试和随机化rollout，共享运动质量诊断套件。训练阶段包含训练稳定化和算法增强以提高优化稳定性和仿真到现实转移。

Result: 在两种硬件平台（Unitree G1和Booster T1）上验证了五种代表性人形技能（运动、恢复、运动模仿、移动操作），实现了一致的仿真到现实转移。

Conclusion: 标准化、端到端的工作流可以显著提高人形机器人强化学习开发的可靠性和可重复性。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning (RL) have enabled impressive humanoid behaviors in simulation, yet transferring these results to new robots remains challenging. In many real deployments, the primary bottleneck is no longer simulation throughput or algorithm design, but the absence of systematic infrastructure that links environment verification, training, evaluation, and deployment in a coherent loop.
  To address this gap, we present AGILE, an end-to-end workflow for humanoid RL that standardizes the policy-development lifecycle to mitigate common sim-to-real failure modes. AGILE comprises four stages: (1) interactive environment verification, (2) reproducible training, (3) unified evaluation, and (4) descriptor-driven deployment via robot/task configuration descriptors. For evaluation stage, AGILE supports both scenario-based tests and randomized rollouts under a shared suite of motion-quality diagnostics, enabling automated regression testing and principled robustness assessment. AGILE also incorporates a set of training stabilizations and algorithmic enhancements in training stage to improve optimization stability and sim-to-real transfer.
  With this pipeline in place, we validate AGILE across five representative humanoid skills spanning locomotion, recovery, motion imitation, and loco-manipulation on two hardware platforms (Unitree G1 and Booster T1), achieving consistent sim-to-real transfer. Overall, AGILE shows that a standardized, end-to-end workflow can substantially improve the reliability and reproducibility of humanoid RL development.

</details>


### [19] [HortiMulti: A Multi-Sensor Dataset for Localisation and Mapping in Horticultural Polytunnels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20150)
*Shuoyuan Xu,Zhipeng Zhong,Tiago Barros,Matthew Coombes,Cristiano Premebida,Hao Wu,Cunjia Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HortiMulti是一个多模态、跨季节的园艺数据集，在商业草莓和覆盆子塑料大棚中采集，包含LiDAR、相机、IMU等多种传感器数据，旨在解决园艺环境中机器人感知和定位算法的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 园艺机器人技术在经济上具有重要意义，但相比城市和林业机器人领域，园艺环境缺乏具有代表性的真实世界数据集。园艺环境存在外观变化大、动态植被、塑料覆盖物反光、感知混淆严重、GNSS不可靠等挑战，这些因素直接影响了现有定位和感知算法的性能。

Method: 在商业草莓和覆盆子塑料大棚中采集整个生长季节的数据，传感器套件包括两个3D LiDAR、四个RGB相机、IMU、GNSS和轮式里程计。使用全站仪测量、AprilTag基准标记和LiDAR惯性里程计的组合获得地面真实轨迹，涵盖密集、稀疏和无标记覆盖，支持在受控和真实条件下的评估。

Result: 发布了时间同步的原始测量数据、校准文件、参考轨迹以及视觉、LiDAR和多传感器SLAM的基准测试。结果表明，当前最先进的方法在塑料大棚部署中仍然不足，无法提供可靠的性能。

Conclusion: HortiMulti为园艺环境中机器人感知系统的开发和测试提供了一个一站式资源，填补了该领域代表性数据集的空白，并证明了现有算法在复杂园艺环境中的局限性。

Abstract: Agricultural robotics is gaining increasing relevance in both research and real-world deployment. As these systems are expected to operate autonomously in more complex tasks, the availability of representative real-world datasets becomes essential. While domains such as urban and forestry robotics benefit from large and established benchmarks, horticultural environments remain comparatively under-explored despite the economic significance of this sector. To address this gap, we present HortiMulti, a multimodal, cross-season dataset collected in commercial strawberry and raspberry polytunnels across an entire growing season, capturing substantial appearance variation, dynamic foliage, specular reflections from plastic covers, severe perceptual aliasing, and GNSS-unreliable conditions, all of which directly degrade existing localisation and perception algorithms. The sensor suite includes two 3D LiDARs, four RGB cameras, an IMU, GNSS, and wheel odometry. Ground truth trajectories are derived from a combination of Total Station surveying, AprilTag fiducial markers, and LiDAR-inertial odometry, spanning dense, sparse, and marker-free coverage to support evaluation under both controlled and realistic conditions. We release time-synchronised raw measurements, calibration files, reference trajectories, and baseline benchmarks for visual, LiDAR, and multi-sensor SLAM, with results confirming that current state-of-the-art methods remain inadequate for reliable polytunnel deployment, establishing HortiMulti as a one-stop resource for developing and testing robotic perception systems in horticulture environments.

</details>


### [20] [The Robot's Inner Critic: Self-Refinement of Social Behaviors through VLM-based Replanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20164)
*Jiyu Lim,Youngwoo Yoon,Kwanghyun Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRISP：基于视觉语言模型的机器人自主社交行为生成框架，通过自我批判和重新规划实现跨平台、类人的社交交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人社交行为生成方法在灵活性和自主性方面受限，依赖于预定义动作或人工反馈。需要一种能够自主评估和改进社交行为、具有跨平台适用性的通用框架。

Method: 提出CRISP框架，利用VLM作为"类人社交批判者"，包含五个核心步骤：1) 分析机器人描述文件提取可动关节和约束；2) 基于情境上下文生成分步行为计划；3) 参考视觉信息（关节活动范围可视化）生成低级关节控制代码；4) VLM评估社交适当性和自然度，识别错误步骤；5) 基于奖励的迭代优化搜索。不依赖特定机器人API，仅需机器人结构文件。

Result: 在包含5种不同类型机器人（移动机械臂和人形机器人）和20个场景的用户研究中，该方法相比先前方法获得了显著更高的偏好评分和情境适当性评分。

Conclusion: CRISP提供了一个通用框架，最小化人工干预的同时扩展了机器人的自主交互能力和跨平台适用性，实现了更自然、更适当的社交行为生成。

Abstract: Conventional robot social behavior generation has been limited in flexibility and autonomy, relying on predefined motions or human feedback. This study proposes CRISP (Critique-and-Replan for Interactive Social Presence), an autonomous framework where a robot critiques and replans its own actions by leveraging a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as a `human-like social critic.' CRISP integrates (1) extraction of movable joints and constraints by analyzing the robot's description file (e.g., MJCF), (2) generation of step-by-step behavior plans based on situational context, (3) generation of low-level joint control code by referencing visual information (joint range-of-motion visualizations), (4) VLM-based evaluation of social appropriateness and naturalness, including pinpointing erroneous steps, and (5) iterative refinement of behaviors through reward-based search. This approach is not tied to a specific robot API; it can generate subtly different, human-like motions on various platforms using only the robot's structure file. In a user study involving five different robot types and 20 scenarios, including mobile manipulators and humanoids, our proposed method achieved significantly higher preference and situational appropriateness ratings compared to previous methods. This research presents a general framework that minimizes human intervention while expanding the robot's autonomous interaction capabilities and cross-platform applicability.
  Detailed result videos and supplementary information regarding this work are available at: https://limjiyu99.github.io/inner-critic/

</details>

### [21] [Diminishing Returns in Expanding Generative Models and Godel-Tarski-Lob Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19687)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个任务空间框架来分析扩展生成推理系统，证明了随着系统容量增加，可解决任务的边际改进必然趋近于零，并揭示了逻辑推理任务存在根本性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成建模系统通过扩大模型容量、训练数据和计算资源来改进，但扩展行为的理论极限仍不清楚。需要建立一个理论框架来分析扩展生成系统的能力增长极限。

Method: 1. 开发了一个通用的任务空间框架，将系统能力定义为在固定任务分布下可成功解决任务的概率质量；2. 在温和假设下证明边际改进收敛到零的结构性结果；3. 基于算法概率的复杂度加权假设类提供预测理论细化；4. 分析逻辑推理任务，应用数学逻辑经典结果（Rosser不完备性、Tarski不可定义性、Löb定理）。

Result: 1. 证明随着系统容量增加，新可解决任务的概率质量必然渐近减小；2. 在预测设置中获得边际改进的定量界限；3. 显示在足够表达力的推理系统中，未解决的逻辑任务将持续存在；4. 表明长期能力增长受到任务覆盖边际改进递减和内部推理根本逻辑限制的双重约束。

Conclusion: 扩展生成系统的渐近行为受到数学约束：能力增长既有任务覆盖边际改进递减的限制，又有内部推理的根本逻辑限制。这为理解生成系统扩展的极限提供了数学视角。

Abstract: Modern generative modelling systems are increasingly improved by expanding model capacity, training data, and computational resources. While empirical studies have documented such scaling behaviour across architectures including generative adversarial networks, variational autoencoders, transformer-based models, and diffusion models, the theoretical limits of capability growth in expanding generative systems remain poorly understood.
  In this paper we develop a general task-space framework for analysing expanding generative reasoning systems. Each system induces a subset of a global task space representing the tasks it can successfully solve, and system capability is measured by the probability mass of this solved-task set under a fixed task distribution. Within this framework we prove a structural result showing that, under mild assumptions, the marginal improvement in solved tasks must converge to zero as system capacity increases. Thus expanding generative systems may continue to gain capability, but the probability mass of newly solvable tasks necessarily diminishes asymptotically.
  We further provide a prediction-theoretic refinement based on complexity-weighted hypothesis classes inspired by algorithmic probability, yielding quantitative bounds on marginal improvement in prediction settings. Finally, we examine logical reasoning tasks and show that classical results from mathematical logic -- including Rosser incompleteness, Tarski's undefinability theorem, and Löb's theorem -- imply the persistence of unresolved logical tasks within sufficiently expressive reasoning systems.
  Together these results provide a mathematical perspective on the asymptotic behaviour of expanding generative systems, showing that long-run capability growth is constrained both by diminishing marginal improvements in task coverage and by fundamental logical limitations on internal reasoning.

</details>


### [22] [How Concise are Chains of co-Büchi Automata?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19806)
*Rüdiger Ehlers*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: COCOA（co-Büchi自动机链）作为ω-正则语言的新规范模型，本文分析其简洁性，发现COCOA在确定性情况下比确定性奇偶自动机指数级更简洁，但布尔运算（析取、合取、补集）会导致指数级膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COCOA作为ω-正则语言的新规范模型，具有多项式时间最小化的优势，但对其与其他自动机模型的关系了解有限。本文旨在分析COCOA的简洁性，特别是研究其在布尔运算中的表现，以评估其在实际应用中的适用性。

Method: 通过理论分析和构造性证明，研究COCOA与确定性奇偶自动机的简洁性比较，分析布尔运算（析取、合取、补集）对COCOA大小的影响。使用语言族构造和复杂度分析来证明指数级膨胀的存在性。

Result: 1. 确定性COCOA比确定性奇偶自动机指数级更简洁；2. 布尔运算（析取、合取）会导致COCOA大小指数级膨胀，而相同操作在确定性奇偶自动机中只需多项式膨胀；3. 补集运算也会导致COCOA指数级膨胀，因为需要重新分配识别颜色。

Conclusion: 虽然COCOA作为ω-正则语言表示具有简洁性优势，但在布尔运算中会遭受指数级膨胀，这限制了其在需要频繁进行布尔运算的应用场景中的实用性。COCOA的简洁性优势与运算代价之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Chains of co-Büchi automata (COCOA) have recently been introduced as a new canonical model for representing arbitrary omega-regular languages. They can be minimized in polynomial time and are hence an attractive language representation for applications in which normally, deterministic omega-automata are used. While it is known how to build COCOA from deterministic parity automata, little is currently known about their relationship to automaton models introduced earlier than COCOA.
  In this paper, we analyze the conciseness of chains of co-Büchi automata. We provide three main results and give an overview of the implications of these results. First of all, we show that even in the case that all automata in the chain are deterministic, chains of co-Büchi automata can be exponentially more concise than deterministic parity automata. We then present two main results that together negatively answer the question if this conciseness is retained when performing Boolean operations (such as disjunction, conjunction, and complementation) over COCOA. For the binary operations, we show that there exist families of languages for which their application leads to an exponential growth of the sizes of the automata. The families have the property that when representing them using deterministic parity automata, taking the disjunction or conjunction of the family elements only requires a polynomial blow-up. We finally show that an exponential blow-up is also unavoidable when complementing a COCOA, as this operation can require redistributing with which colors words need to be recognized.

</details>


### [23] [Dynamically Reprogrammable Runtime Monitors for Bounded-time MTL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19851)
*Chirantan Hebballi,Akash Poptani,Amrutha Benny,Rajshekar Kalayappan,Sandeep Chandran,Ramchandra Phawade*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于标准单元而非FPGA的可重编程运行时验证监控器，支持在线、实时验证动态变化的属性，能够与待验证系统同芯片集成，实现高速监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于FPGA的运行时验证框架虽然支持现场操作期间属性动态变化，但难以跟上高性能处理器的速度。需要一种能够与待验证系统同芯片集成、支持实时监控动态属性的解决方案。

Method: 设计基于标准单元的可重编程监控器，包含实现五种基本操作的可编程单元和一组队列更新规则。通过组合这些可编程单元来忠实实现离散时间、有界MTL（度量时序逻辑）。

Result: 仿真表明监控器可通过I/O引脚在部署后重新编程。支持最多16个原子命题的MTL公式的大型监控器仅占用0.55 mm²面积，工作频率达1.25 GHz。

Conclusion: 提出的基于标准单元的可重编程监控器能够有效解决现有FPGA方案在高速监控方面的局限性，实现与待验证系统同芯片集成，支持动态属性变化的实时验证。

Abstract: A Runtime Verification (RV) framework that supports online, at-speed verification of properties that can change dynamically (during in-field operations) will benefit a large variety of applications. Several state-of-the-art RV frameworks propose to implement monitors on FPGAs. While this approach can support changes to the property being monitored during in-field operations, they struggle to keep pace with the system under verification which use high-performance processors. In this work, we propose a novel, reprogrammable monitor that is implemented using standard cells instead of FPGAs. This allows the monitor to be co-located with the system under verification (on the same die), and hence is amenable to at-speed monitoring of properties. Our proposed design consists of a programmable unit that implements five basic operations and a set of queue-update rules. We show that a composition of such programmable units faithfully implements discrete time, bounded MTL. We demonstrate through simulations that our proposed monitor can be reprogrammed (through its I/O pins) post deployment. A fairly large monitor which can support MTL formulae upto 16 atomic propositions occupies only 0.55 mm^2, while operating at a frequency of 1.25 GHz.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

### [24] [CDEoH: Category-Driven Automatic Algorithm Design With Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19284)
*Yu-Nian Wang,Shen-Huan Lyu,Ning Chen,Jia-Le Xu,Baoliu Ye,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: CDEoH：基于大语言模型的类别驱动自动算法设计，通过显式建模算法类别并平衡性能与类别多样性，解决LLM启发式搜索中的不稳定和早熟收敛问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的启发式搜索方法在进化过程中存在不稳定和早熟收敛问题，现有方法主要通过提示工程或联合进化思想和代码来解决，但忽视了算法类别多样性对维持进化稳定性的关键作用。

Method: 提出CDEoH方法，显式建模算法类别，在种群管理中联合平衡性能和类别多样性，实现跨多个算法范式的并行探索。

Result: 在多个尺度的代表性组合优化问题上进行广泛实验，CDEoH有效缓解了向单一进化方向的收敛，显著增强了进化稳定性，在任务和尺度上实现了一致优越的平均性能。

Conclusion: CDEoH通过显式建模算法类别并平衡性能与多样性，解决了LLM启发式搜索中的不稳定问题，实现了更稳定和有效的自动算法设计。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), LLM-based heuristic search methods have demonstrated strong capabilities in automated algorithm generation. However, their evolutionary processes often suffer from instability and premature convergence. Existing approaches mainly address this issue through prompt engineering or by jointly evolving thought and code, while largely overlooking the critical role of algorithmic category diversity in maintaining evolutionary stability. To this end, we propose Category Driven Automatic Algorithm Design with Large Language Models (CDEoH), which explicitly models algorithm categories and jointly balances performance and category diversity in population management, enabling parallel exploration across multiple algorithmic paradigms. Extensive experiments on representative combinatorial optimization problems across multiple scales demonstrate that CDEoH effectively mitigates convergence toward a single evolutionary direction, significantly enhancing evolutionary stability and achieving consistently superior average performance across tasks and scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [25] [Goedel-Code-Prover: Hierarchical Proof Search for Open State-of-the-Art Code Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19329)
*Zenan Li,Ziran Yang,Deyuan,He,Haoyu Zhao,Andrew Zhao,Shange Tang,Kaiyu Yang,Aarti Gupta,Zhendong Su,Chi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出分层证明搜索框架，通过结构分解和强化学习训练统一策略，在Lean代码验证基准上实现62%成功率，比基线提升2.6倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的代码缺乏正确性保证，形式化验证需要构建机器可检查的证明，当前自动化能力有限。需要开发能够分解复杂验证目标的方法，提高代码验证的自动化水平。

Method: 提出分层证明搜索框架，将复杂验证目标分解为结构更简单的子目标。核心是结合构造性证明和结构有效性的分解评分机制，该评分同时作为训练奖励和推理时排序标准。训练Goedel-Code-Prover-8B统一策略，采用监督初始化后接混合强化学习，连续分解奖励驱动规划探索，监督回放稳定证明生成。

Result: 在包含427个任务的三个Lean代码验证基准上，8B参数模型达到62.0%的证明成功率，比最强基线提升2.6倍，超越最大达84倍参数的神经证明器。推理时扩展性一致：成功率随搜索迭代和采样预算单调提升，训练模型在可比规模下比前沿现成模型更高效。

Conclusion: 分层证明搜索框架通过结构分解和强化学习训练的统一策略，显著提升了代码验证的自动化能力。分解评分机制确保了训练和推理的一致性，混合强化学习方法平衡了探索和稳定性。该方法在效率和效果上均优于现有方法，展示了神经证明器在形式化验证中的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate plausible code but offer limited guarantees of correctness. Formally verifying that implementations satisfy specifications requires constructing machine-checkable proofs, a task that remains beyond current automation. We propose a hierarchical proof search framework for automated code verification in Lean~4 that decomposes complex verification goals into structurally simpler subgoals before attempting tactic-level proving. Central to our approach is a principled decomposition score that combines constructive justification with structural effectiveness. Crucially, this score serves as both the training reward and the inference-time ranking criterion, ensuring strict alignment between optimization and deployment. We train Goedel-Code-Prover-8B, a single unified policy for both decomposition and completion, via supervised initialization followed by hybrid reinforcement learning, where a continuous decomposition reward drives planning exploration while supervised replay stabilizes proof generation. On three Lean-based code verification benchmarks comprising 427 tasks, our 8B-parameter model achieves a 62.0\% prove success rate, a 2.6$\times$ improvement over the strongest baseline, surpassing neural provers up to 84$\times$ larger. We further observe consistent inference-time scaling: success rates improve monotonically with search iterations and sampling budget, with our trained model achieving greater efficiency than frontier off-the-shelf models of comparable scale.

</details>


### [26] [DePro: Understanding the Role of LLMs in Debugging Competitive Programming Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19399)
*Nabiha Parvez,Tanvin Sarkar Pallab,Mia Mohammad Imran,Tarannum Shaila Zaman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DePro：一种基于测试用例驱动的LLM调试方法，通过暴力参考生成、压力测试和迭代LLM引导精炼来高效修复竞争编程中的错误代码，相比人类程序员和零样本LLM调试显著减少调试尝试和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调试在软件开发周期中占用大量时间，但大型语言模型（LLMs）在此任务中的有效性尚未得到充分理解。竞争编程因其多样化问题领域和严格效率要求，为此类评估提供了丰富的基准。

Method: DePro采用测试用例驱动的方法，通过纠正现有代码而非生成新解决方案来辅助程序员。该方法结合：1）暴力参考生成创建正确参考实现；2）压力测试识别错误；3）迭代LLM引导精炼来高效定位和修复错误。

Result: 在Codeforces的13个错误用户提交上进行实验，DePro始终产生正确解决方案，相比人类程序员和零样本LLM调试：调试尝试减少高达64%，每个问题的平均调试时间减少7.6分钟。

Conclusion: DePro展示了LLM在竞争编程调试任务中的有效性，通过系统化的测试用例驱动方法显著提升了调试效率，为LLM辅助软件开发提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Debugging consumes a substantial portion of the software development lifecycle, yet the effectiveness of Large Language Models(LLMs) in this task is not well understood. Competitive programming offers a rich benchmark for such evaluation, given its diverse problem domains and strict efficiency requirements. We present an empirical study of LLM-based debugging on competitive programming problems and introduce DePro, a test-case driven approach that assists programmers by correcting existing code rather than generating new solutions. DePro combines brute-force reference generation, stress testing, and iterative LLM-guided refinement to identify and resolve errors efficiently.Experiments on 13 faulty user submissions from Codeforces demonstrate that DePro consistently produces correct solutions, reducing debugging attempts by up to 64% and debugging time by an average of 7.6 minutes per problem compared to human programmers and zero-shot LLM debugging.

</details>


### [27] [Coordinating Stakeholders in the Consideration of Performance Indicators and Respective Interface Requirements for Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19492)
*Richard Schubert,Marvin Loba,Alexander Blödel,David Klüner,Alexandru Kampmann,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个协调利益相关者考虑自动驾驶汽车性能指标和接口要求的流程，确保系统自感知实现的可追溯性和有效沟通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使SAE L4级自动驾驶车辆能够处理外部干扰和内部退化/故障，需要实现自感知和自适应系统。缺乏系统化的利益相关者协调流程会导致架构决策不可追溯，利益相关者间沟通效率低下。

Method: 提出一个流程导向的方法，包含必要的要素、步骤和工件，明确解决利益相关者沟通、可追溯性和知识转移问题。该方法基于在autotech.agil项目中的实践经验。

Result: 开发了一个系统化的协调流程，能够明确记录性能指标和接口要求，确保架构决策的可追溯性。从项目应用中获得了经验教训，识别了现有差距。

Conclusion: 提出的流程方法对于实现自动驾驶车辆的自感知和自适应能力至关重要，能够有效协调利益相关者并确保决策的可追溯性。未来工作需要进一步解决已识别的差距。

Abstract: This paper presents a process for coordinating stakeholders in their consideration of performance indicators and respective interface requirements for automated vehicles. These performance indicators are obtained and processed based on the system's self-perception and enable the realization of self-aware and self-adaptive vehicles. This is necessary to allow SAE Level 4 vehicles to handle external disturbances as well as internal degradations and failures at runtime. Without such a systematic process for stakeholder coordination, architectural decisions on realizing self-perception become untraceable and effective communication between stakeholders may be compromised. Our process-oriented approach includes necessary ingredients, steps, and artifacts that explicitly address stakeholder communication, traceability, and knowledge transfer through clear documentation. Our approach is based on the experience gained from applying the process in the autotech.agil project, from which we further present lessons learned, identified gaps, and steps for future work.

</details>


### [28] [GazePrinter: Visualizing Expert Gaze to Guide Novices in a New Codebase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19855)
*Peng Kuang,Emma Söderberg,April Yi Wang,Martin Höst*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GazePrinter通过可视化专家凝视模式来帮助新手程序员理解代码，研究发现它能引导新手更接近专家的代码阅读路径，并可能减少时间和认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序理解是软件工程中的关键活动，对新手尤其具有挑战性。尽管凝视作为新兴模态已被用于研究程序员视觉注意力和认知过程，但将基于凝视的辅助集成到开发环境中以支持程序员的研究还很有限。同时，联合注意力在协作中的重要性表明探索集体凝视具有价值。

Method: 开发了GazePrinter工具，通过提供基于专家凝视的视觉线索来辅助新手程序理解。采用混合方法研究，包括调查、控制实验和访谈，对40名新手程序员进行了研究。

Result: 专家凝视可视化对新手程序员的行为有显著影响：使用GazePrinter的新手在代码库中采取的路径更接近专家的路径。同时发现了使用GazePrinter的新手在时间和认知负荷方面减少的迹象。

Conclusion: 可视化专家凝视模式可以有效引导新手程序员的代码理解过程，使其更接近专家的阅读路径，并可能降低认知负担。这为基于凝视的程序理解辅助工具开发提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Program comprehension is an essential activity in software engineering. Not only does it often challenge professionals, but it can also hinder novices from advancing their programming skills. Gaze, an emerging modality in developer tools, has so far primarily been utilized to improve our understanding of programmers' visual attention and as a means to reason about programmers' cognitive processes. There has been limited exploration of integrating gaze-based assistance into development environments to support programmers, despite the tight links between attention and gaze. We also know that joint attention is important in collaboration, further suggesting that there is value in exploring collective gaze.
  In this paper, we investigate the effect of visualizing gaze patterns gathered from experts to novice programmers to assist them with program comprehension in a new codebase. To this end, we present GazePrinter, designed to provide gaze-orienting visual cues informed by experts to aid novices with program comprehension. We present the results of a mixed-methods study conducted with 40 novices to study the effects of using GazePrinter for program comprehension tasks. The study included a survey, a controlled experiment, and interviews. We found that visualization of expert gaze can have a significant effect on novice programmers' behavior in terms of which path they take through the code base; with GazePrinter, novices took a path closer to the path taken by experts. We also found indications of reduced time and cognitive load among novices using GazePrinter.

</details>


### [29] [Revealing Domain-Spatiality Patterns for Configuration Tuning: Domain Knowledge Meets Fitness Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19897)
*Yulong Ye,Hongyuan Liang,Chao Jiang,Miqing Li,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Domland结合适应度景观分析和领域知识，系统性地解释配置调优案例的隐藏特征，揭示调优器成功或失败的原因，为调优器设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置调优对软件质量保证至关重要，但调优器有效性的评估存在黑盒问题。现有静态领域分析方法缺乏普适性，动态数据分析方法解释性不足，需要一种能够连接领域知识和调优难度的桥梁方法。

Method: 提出Domland方法，采用双管齐下的方法论，将适应度景观分析(FLA)获取的空间信息与领域驱动分析相结合，系统性地捕捉配置调优案例的隐藏特征，解释调优器成功或失败的原因。

Result: 通过对9个软件系统和93个工作负载的案例研究，发现：(1)配置景观具有系统特异性，没有单一领域因素能一致塑造其结构；(2)控制主要功能流程的核心选项比资源选项对景观崎岖度影响更大；(3)工作负载对景观结构的影响与类型或规模没有统一关联，其影响是系统依赖的。

Conclusion: Domland方法能够有效解释和情境化调优器行为，为调优器设计提供信息。配置调优的难度受系统特异性、核心选项和工作负载的复杂交互影响，需要针对具体系统进行深入分析。

Abstract: Configuration tuning for better performance is crucial in quality assurance. Yet, there has long been a mystery on tuners' effectiveness, due to the black-box nature of configurable systems. Prior efforts predominantly adopt static domain analysis (e.g., static taint analysis), which often lacks generalizability, or dynamic data analysis (e.g., benchmarking performance analysis), limiting explainability. In this work, we embrace Fitness Landscape Analysis (FLA) as a bridge between domain knowledge and difficulty of the tuning. We propose Domland, a two-pronged methodology that synergizes the spatial information obtained from FLA and domain-driven analysis to systematically capture the hidden characteristics of configuration tuning cases, explaining how and why a tuner might succeed or fail. This helps to better interpret and contextualize the behavior of tuners and inform tuner design. To evaluate Domland, we conduct a case study of nine software systems and 93 workloads, from which we reveal several key findings: (1) configuration landscapes are inherently system-specific, with no single domain factor (e.g., system area, programming language, or resource intensity) consistently shaping their structure; (2) the core options (e.g., pic-struct of x264), which control the main functional flows, exert a stronger influence on landscape ruggedness (i.e. the difficulty of tuning) compared to resource options (e.g., cpu-independent of x264); (3) Workload effects on landscape structure are not uniformly tied to type or scale. Both contribute to landscape variations, but their impact is system-dependent.

</details>


### [30] [Teaching Practically Relevant Research Problem Formulation in Software Engineering with Lean Research Inception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19967)
*Anrafel Fernandes Pereira,Tatiane Ornelas,Allysson Allex Araujo,Marcos Kalinowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LRI方法支持软件工程学生制定与实践对齐的研究问题，在巴西大学案例研究中显示积极效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究中良好定义的研究问题对弥合产业界与学术界之间的鸿沟至关重要，但学生往往难以制定与实践对齐的研究问题

Method: 在巴西大学进行案例研究，涉及60名学生和7名教师顾问，应用精益研究启动（LRI）方法支持学生制定研究问题

Result: 学生报告在推理（60%）、清晰度和定义（61.7%）、情境化（60%）和沟通（50%）方面受益；教师观察到更清晰结构化的问题（57.1%），推荐率高达85.7%

Conclusion: LRI是支持软件工程教育中制定与实践对齐研究问题的有前景方法

Abstract: [Background] Well-formulated Software Engineering (SE) research problems are essential for bridging the gap between industry-academia. Lean Research Inception (LRI) aims to support this activity. [Goal] Apply LRI to support SE students in formulating practice-aligned research problems. [Method] We conducted a case study with 60 students and 7 faculty advisors of a Brazilian university. [Results] Students reported benefits in reasoning (60%), clarity and definition (61.7%), contextualization (60%), and communication (50%). Advisors also observed clearer and more structured problems (57.1%) with a high recommendation rate (85.7%). [Conclusion] LRI can be a promising approach to support practice-aligned research problem formulation in SE education.

</details>


### [31] [Agentic Harness for Real-World Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20075)
*Yingwei Zheng,Cong Li,Shaohua Li,Yuqun Zhang,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: llvm-autofix是首个专为修复编译器bug设计的智能体框架，针对LLVM编译器基础设施，通过专用工具、基准测试和定制化智能体显著提升LLM修复编译器bug的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译器bug修复困难，传统LLM方法在处理编译器bug时面临独特挑战：复杂度高、需要跨领域专业知识、bug报告稀疏且描述不充分，需要专门的编译器修复工具。

Method: 开发llvm-autofix框架，包含：1）智能体友好的LLVM工具集；2）可复现的LLVM bug基准测试llvm-bench；3）专门修复LLVM bug的最小化智能体llvm-autofix-mini。

Result: 前沿模型在处理编译器bug时性能下降60%；llvm-autofix-mini比现有最优方法提升约22%的性能，证明专用框架的必要性。

Conclusion: llvm-autofix为LLM在复杂系统（如编译器）中的应用奠定了基础，强调需要专门设计的工具来弥合LLM与编译器工程之间的差距。

Abstract: Compilers are critical to modern computing, yet fixing compiler bugs is difficult. While recent large language model (LLM) advancements enable automated bug repair, compiler bugs pose unique challenges due to their complexity, deep cross-domain expertise requirements, and sparse, non-descriptive bug reports, necessitating compiler-specific tools. To bridge the gap, we introduce llvm-autofix, the first agentic harness designed to assist LLM agents in understanding and fixing compiler bugs. Our focus is on LLVM, one of the most widely used compiler infrastructures. Central to llvm-autofix are agent-friendly LLVM tools, a benchmark llvm-bench of reproducible LLVM bugs, and a tailored minimal agent llvm-autofix-mini for fixing LLVM bugs. Our evaluation demonstrates a performance decline of 60% in frontier models when tackling compiler bugs compared with common software bugs. Our minimal agent llvm-autofix-mini also outperforms the state-of-the-art by approximately 22%. This emphasizes the necessity for specialized harnesses like ours to close the loop between LLMs and compiler engineering. We believe this work establishes a foundation for advancing LLM capabilities in complex systems like compilers. GitHub: https://github.com/dtcxzyw/llvm-autofix

</details>

### [32] [Physical Layer Message Prediction for 5G Radio Access Network Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19760)
*Jonathan Ebert,Peter Rost*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 基于Transformer的5G NR协议逆向工程框架，利用5G标准上下文和语法预测物理层后续消息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协议逆向工程是安全研究的前沿方法，需要开发能够逆向分析移动通信系统通信的框架，特别是针对3GPP发布的5G NR系统

Method: 利用5G标准的可用上下文和语法，基于Transformer模型预测后续消息；使用开源5G系统实现（模拟基站和多个用户设备）进行训练，预测目标为物理层消息

Result: 开发了一个能够逆向工程5G NR通信的框架，通过Transformer模型成功预测物理层后续消息

Conclusion: 提出的框架在5G NR协议逆向工程中具有应用潜力，为移动通信系统安全研究提供了新方法

Abstract: Protocol reverse engineering stands as the cutting-edge approach in security research. This paper presents a framework capable of reverse engineering the communications within a mobile communication system. Our focus is on systems released by the 3GPP, with an emphasis on 5G NR.
  Our approach leverages the available context and syntax of the 5G standard to predict subsequent messages. This approach relies on a Transformer model and is trained based on an open-source 5G system implementation, emulating a base station and several user equipments. The prediction targets messages at the physical layer.

</details>


### [33] [Implementing the L4S Architecture in the ns-3 Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20166)
*Maria Eduarda Veras,Eduardo Freitas,Assis T. de Oliveira Filho,Djamel Sadok,Judith Kelner*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 在ns-3中实现L4S架构的端到端协议，包括从Linux内核移植TCP Prague和集成AccECN，解决了Linux数据包算法与ns-3字节架构的兼容性问题，验证了模型准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用（如云游戏、增强现实）对超低延迟的需求暴露了传统拥塞控制算法在缓冲区膨胀方面的局限性。虽然Linux内核已有L4S实现，但研究社区缺乏完整的ns-3高保真模型进行可重复实验。

Method: 在ns-3中实现L4S架构的端到端协议，重点包括：1）从Linux内核v6.12移植TCP Prague协议；2）集成Accurate ECN（AccECN）信令；3）解决Linux数据包算法与ns-3字节架构的兼容性工程挑战，特别是窗口管理和数据包调度的算法转换。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的模型能够准确复现在实际测试场景中观察到的拥塞响应行为，验证了平台的准确性。该实现为研究社区提供了在受控环境中进行复杂L4S性能评估的验证工具集。

Conclusion: 本文成功在ns-3中实现了L4S架构的端到端协议，解决了从Linux内核移植到ns-3模拟器的重要工程挑战，为研究社区提供了高质量、可重复的L4S性能评估平台。

Abstract: The demand for ultra-low latency in modern applications, such as cloud gaming and augmented reality, has exposed the limitations of traditional congestion control algorithms regarding bufferbloat. The Low Latency, Low Loss, and Scalable Throughput (L4S) architecture addresses this challenge by combining scalable congestion controls, such as TCP Prague, low-latency queue management with prioritization, and Accurate ECN (AccECN) feedback. Although Linux kernel implementations exist, the research community lacks a complete, high-fidelity model within the Network Simulator 3 (ns-3) for reproducible experiments. This paper presents an implementation of end-host protocols for the L4S architecture in ns-3, focusing on the porting of TCP Prague from the Linux kernel (v6.12) and the integration of AccECN signaling. Significant engineering challenges regarding the adaptation of kernel logic are detailed, particularly the reconciliation of Linux's packet-based arithmetic with ns-3's byte-based architecture for window management and pacing. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed model faithfully reproduces the congestion response behaviors observed in real-world testbed scenarios, validating the platform's accuracy. Consequently, this work provides the community with a validated toolset for complex L4S performance evaluations in controlled environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [34] [Planning Autonomous Vehicle Maneuvering in Work Zones Through Game-Theoretic Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19556)
*Mayar Nour,Atrisha Sarkar,Mohamed H. Zaki*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出基于博弈论的轨迹生成与控制框架，用于提升自动驾驶车辆在工作区换道操作的安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作区导航是自动驾驶最具挑战性的操作之一，受限几何形状和不可预测的交通模式造成高风险环境，现有研究缺乏针对工作区安全决策的专门方法

Method: 将换道操作建模为车辆间的非合作博弈，使用博弈论规划器生成平衡安全性、进度和交通稳定性的轨迹

Result: 仿真结果显示，与传统车辆行为规划模型相比，所提博弈论模型在安全关键的高速公路工作区场景中减少35%的冲突频率，并降低高风险安全事件的概率

Conclusion: 博弈论框架能有效提升自动驾驶车辆在工作区环境中的换道安全性，为复杂交通场景下的决策规划提供了新思路

Abstract: Work zone navigation remains one of the most challenging manoeuvres for autonomous vehicles (AVs), where constrained geometries and unpredictable traffic patterns create a high-risk environment. Despite extensive research on AV trajectory planning, few studies address the decision-making required to navigate work zones safely. This paper proposes a novel game-theoretic framework for trajectory generation and control to enhance the safety of lane changes in a work zone environment. By modelling the lane change manoeuvre as a non-cooperative game between vehicles, we use a game-theoretic planner to generate trajectories that balance safety, progress, and traffic stability. The simulation results show that the proposed game-theoretic model reduces the frequency of conflicts by 35 percent and decreases the probability of high risk safety events compared to traditional vehicle behaviour planning models in safety-critical highway work-zone scenarios.

</details>


### [35] [Helix: A Dual-Helix Co-Evolutionary Multi-Agent System for Prompt Optimization and Question Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19732)
*Kewen Zhu,Liping Yi,Zhiming Zhao,Xiang Li,Qinghua Hu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Helix：一个通过三阶段协同进化框架联合优化问题重构和提示指令的多智能体系统，在12个基准测试中相比6个基线方法实现了最高3.95%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动提示优化方法受限于固定模板、有限搜索空间或单边优化，将用户问题视为不可变输入。实际上问题表述和提示设计是相互依存的：清晰的问题结构有助于聚焦推理和任务理解，而有效的提示能揭示更好的问题组织方式。忽略这种耦合关系从根本上限制了当前APO方法的有效性和适应性。

Method: 提出Helix统一多智能体系统，通过结构化三阶段协同进化框架联合优化问题重构和提示指令：1）规划器引导的分解，将优化分解为耦合的问题-提示目标；2）双轨协同进化，专业智能体迭代优化和相互批判以产生互补改进；3）策略驱动的问题生成，实例化高质量重构以实现鲁棒推理。

Result: 在12个基准测试上与6个强基线方法对比，Helix展示了有效性，在各项任务中实现了高达3.95%的性能提升，并具有有利的优化效率。

Conclusion: Helix通过联合优化问题重构和提示指令，克服了现有自动提示优化方法的局限性，实现了显著的性能提升，证明了问题表述与提示设计协同优化的重要性。

Abstract: Automated prompt optimization (APO) aims to improve large language model performance by refining prompt instructions. However, existing methods are largely constrained by fixed prompt templates, limited search spaces, or single-sided optimization that treats user questions as immutable inputs. In practice, question formulation and prompt design are inherently interdependent: clearer question structures facilitate focused reasoning and task understanding, while effective prompts reveal better ways to organize and restate queries. Ignoring this coupling fundamentally limits the effectiveness and adaptability of current APO approaches. We propose a unified multi-agent system (Helix) that jointly optimizes question reformulation and prompt instructions through a structured three-stage co-evolutionary framework. Helix integrates (1) planner-guided decomposition that breaks optimization into coupled question-prompt objectives, (2) dual-track co-evolution where specialized agents iteratively refine and critique each other to produce complementary improvements, and (3) strategy-driven question generation that instantiates high-quality reformulations for robust inference. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmarks against 6 strong baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of Helix, achieving up to 3.95% performance improvements across tasks with favorable optimization efficiency.

</details>

### [36] [Incremental Live Programming via Shortcut Memoization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19560)
*Marisa Kirisame,Thomas J. Porter,Ruqing Yang,Jianqiu Zhao,Yudi Wu,Ivan Wei,Cyrus Omar,Pavel Panchekha*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Chordata是一个基于捷径记忆化的增量程序解释器，通过观察先前程序版本的执行来学习重复计算模式，并在重新执行相同或结构相似的程序片段时应用这些捷径，显著提升实时编程系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时编程系统需要在程序编辑后快速显示动态影响，但重新执行大型或复杂程序面临计算挑战，因此需要增量式解释器实现来提高效率。

Method: 基于捷径记忆化技术，通过观察先前程序版本的执行学习重复计算模式（捷径），并在重新执行相同或结构相似的程序片段时应用这些捷径。提供形式化语义和机械化证明，并在Hazel实时编程系统中实现，开发实用启发式方法来学习高价值捷径。

Result: 在学生解决简单编程问题的编辑轨迹上评估，相比基线获得13.03倍加速，内存开销为19.97倍。对于较小更改和更复杂程序，获得更大加速。在单次执行内也能提供加速，且输入越大加速越快。

Conclusion: Chordata通过捷径记忆化有效解决了实时编程系统中的增量执行问题，显著提升性能，特别适用于复杂程序和频繁小修改场景，为实时编程系统提供了实用的增量解释器实现方案。

Abstract: Live programming systems aim to quickly show programmers the dynamic impacts of program edits. To do so, they re-execute the program whenever it is edited, which poses a computational challenge when programs become large or complex. This has led to the need for incrementality in the implementation of live program interpreters. This paper introduces Chordata, an incremental program interpreter based on shortcut memoization, which learns repeated patterns of computation, called shortcuts, by observing executions of previous versions of a program. It can then apply these shortcuts when the same or a structurally similar program fragment is re-executed. This paper contributes a formal semantics of shortcut memoization for any language with a rewrite-based semantics, with mechanized proofs of key correctness properties. We then express a variant of the Hazel live programming system, expressed as a CEK machine, in Chordata, and develop a number of practical heuristics to learn high-value shortcuts. We evaluate the resulting system on editing traces of students solving simple programming problems. Chordata achieves a speedup of 13.03\times compared to baseline with a 19.97\times memory overhead. For smaller changes and for more complex programs, Chordata achieves even greater speedups. Furthermore, we show that Chordata is capable of providing a speedup even within a single execution, with a faster speedup on a larger input.

</details>
